pitch_one = tile;\n    pitch = 1;\n    last_pitch = 1;\n  } else if (sound_timer.get_timegone() < 0.02f) {\n    pitch = last_pitch;\n  } else {\n    switch ((pitch_one - tile) % 7) {\n      case -6:\n        pitch = 1.f/2;  // C.\n        break;\n      case -5:\n        pitch = 5.f/8;  // E.\n        break;\n      case -4:\n        pitch = 4.f/6;  // F.\n        break;\n      case -3:\n        pitch = 3.f/4;  // G.\n        break;\n      case -2:\n        pitch = 5.f/6;  // A.\n        break;\n      case -1:\n        pitch = 9.f/10;  // Bb.\n        break;\n      case 0:\n        pitch = 1.f;  // c.\n        break;\n      case 1:\n        pitch = 9.f/8;  // d.\n        break;\n      case 2:\n        pitch = 5.f/4;  // e.\n        break;\n      case 3:\n        pitch = 4.f/3;  // f.\n        break;\n      case 4:\n        pitch = 3.f/2;  // g.\n        break;\n      case 5:\n        pitch = 5.f/3;  // a.\n        break;\n      case 6:\n        pitch = 9.f/5;  // bb.\n        break;\n    }\n    last_pitch = pitch;\n  }\n  sound_timer.start(1);\n\n  std::unique_ptr<SoundSource> soundSource = SoundManager::current()->create_sound_source(\"sounds/coin.wav\");\n  soundSource->set_position(get_pos());\n  soundSource->set_pitch(pitch);\n  soundSource->play();\n  SoundManager::current()->manage_source(std::move(soundSource));\n\n  Sector::get().get_players()[0]->get_status().add_coins(1, false);\n  Sector::get().add<BouncyCoin>(get_pos(), false, get_sprite_name());\n  if (m_count_stats && !m_parent_dispenser)\n    Sector::get().get_level().m_stats.increment_coins();\n  remove_me();\n\n  if (!m_collect_script.empty()) {\n    Sector::get().run_script(m_collect_script, \"collect-script\");\n  }\n}\n\nHitResponse\nCoin::collision(MovingObject& other, const CollisionHit& )\n{\n  auto player = dynamic_cast<Player*>(&other);\n  if (player == nullptr)\n    return ABORT_MOVE;\n  if (m_col.get_bbox().overlaps(player->get_bbox().grown(-0.1f)))\n    collect();\n  return ABORT_MOVE;\n}\n\n/* The following defines a coin subject to gravity. */\nHeavyCoin::HeavyCoin(const Vector& pos, CollisionHit& hit)\n{\n  float clink_threshold = 100.0f; // Sets the minimum speed needed to result in collision noise.\n  // TODO: Colliding with HeavyCoins should have their own unique sound.\n\n  if (hit.bottom) {\n    if (m_physic.get_velocity_y() > clink_threshold && !m_last_hit.bottom)\n        SoundManager::current()->play(\"sounds/coin2.ogg\", get_pos());\n    if (m_physic.get_velocity_y() > 200) {// lets some coins bounce\n      m_physic.set_velocity_y(-99);\n    } else {\n      m_physic.set_velocity_y(0);\n    }\n  }\n  if (hit.right || hit.left) {\n    if ((m_physic.get_velocity_x() > clink_threshold ||\n         m_physic.get_velocity_x()< -clink_threshold) &&\n         hit.right != m_last_hit.right && hit.left != m_last_hit.left)\n      SoundManager::current()->play(\"sounds/coin2.ogg\", get_pos());\n    m_physic.set_velocity_x(-m_physic.get_velocity_x());\n  }\n  if (hit.top) {\n    if (m_physic.get_velocity_y() < -clink_threshold && !m_last_hit.top)\n      SoundManager::current()->play(\"sounds/coin2.ogg\", get_pos());\n    m_physic.set_velocity_y(-m_physic.get_velocity_y());\n  }\n\n  // Only make a sound if the coin wasn't hittin anything last frame (A coin\n  // stuck in solid matter would flood the sound manager - see #1555 on GitHub).\n  m_last_hit = hit;\n}\n\nvoid\nCoin::move_to(const Vector& pos)\n{\n  Vector These fixes come from reading the code, and the sink speed in particular is a guess. The game builds cleanly, but I haven't played through a flooded level to check the result.\n\n**What I fixed**\n1. **Half the coins flew downward** (`src/object/coin_explode.cpp`). Each coin's throw is meant to have a mirror image on the left side. The code flipped the vertical direction instead of the horizontal one, so half the coins went straight down instead of fanning out upward to both sides.\n2. **Coins never stopped sliding** (`src/object/coin.cpp`, `HeavyCoin::collision_solid`). When a coin landed and stopped bouncing, only its vertical speed was zeroed, so it kept sliding along the floor. Its horizontal speed is now zeroed too, so it comes to rest.\n3. **Coins ignored water** (`src/object/coin.cpp`, `HeavyCoin::update`). Released coins used normal air physics underwater, so they dropped straight through. Now, while a coin overlaps water:\n   - it is slowed sharply in every direction except down, so a coin thrown upward underwater rises only about 80 px before stopping;\n   - it sinks at a fixed 80 px/s however fast it hit the surface, so it doesn't bounce or clink on the basin floor.\n\n**What to check**\n- **The sink speed.** I wanted to set it from the basin in the video, but I couldn't. The one bundled level with an explode block (`world2/flooded_chambers.stl`) isn't that basin, so I assumed the block sits about 5 tiles (160 px) above the floor. At 80 px/s that takes about 2 seconds. If your basin is a different depth, change `WATER_SINK_SPEED` in `coin.cpp` to that height in pixels divided by 2.\n- **Other faults.** If the recording shows something else, such as a problem with collecting coins or with the falling-coins (\"rain\") block, I'll have missed it.
